Using COVID-19 disinformation as a first proof-by-example setting, we construct a reference dataset of 700 modulated items, drawn from twenty base sentences across five modulation levels and seven strategies. We then run two linked evaluations with seven different language models: one testing for interpretation through meaning recovery and one for disinformation detection through classification. Curve fitting over modulation levels yields an estimate of the Majority Understandable Modulation threshold and enables sensitivity analyses across strategies and models, see Figure 1. Results reveal the characteristic relationships between understandability and modulation. This study lays the groundwork for understanding the dynamics behind Algospeak and provides the framework, dataset, and experimental setups described.

</details>


### [145] [Cited but Not Verified: Parsing and Evaluating Source Attribution in LLM Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06635)
*Hailey Onweller,Elias Lumer,Austin Huber,Pia Ramchandani,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Corey Feld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向LLM生成报告的内联引用归因评估框架，通过可复现的AST解析器提取并评估Markdown报告中的引用。该框架沿三个维度（链接可用性、内容相关性、事实准确性）进行评估，发现即使最先进的模型事实准确性也仅为39-77%，且随着检索深度增加准确性平均下降42%，揭示了表面引用质量与事实可靠性之间的严重脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM生成的研究报告引用无法可靠验证：现有方法要么依赖模型自我引用（存在偏见风险），要么使用RAG但不验证源可访问性、相关性和事实一致性，缺乏系统化的引用归因评估手段。

Method: 提出基于AST解析器的引用归因评估框架：1) 使用可复现的AST解析器从LLM生成的Markdown报告中提取内联引用；2) 通过检索实际被引内容，沿三个维度评估引用质量——(1) Link Works验证URL可访问性，(2) Relevant Content衡量主题对齐度，(3) Fact Check验证事实准确性；3) 使用基于人类校准的LLM-as-a-judge评分器评估14个闭源和开源模型。

Result: 最强前沿模型链接有效性>94%、相关性>80%，但事实准确性仅39-77%；不到一半的开源模型能在一轮生成中成功生成带引用的报告；消融实验显示，工具调用从2次扩展到150次时，两个前沿模型的事实准确性平均下降约42%。

Conclusion: 表面引用质量与事实可靠性之间存在严重脱节，更多检索并不产生更准确的引用；本框架提供了评估这一脱节的必要基础设施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) power deep research agents that synthesize information from hundreds of web sources into cited reports, yet these citations cannot be reliably verified. Current approaches either trust models to self-cite accurately, risking bias, or employ retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that does not validate source accessibility, relevance, or factual consistency. We introduce the first source attribution evaluation framework that uses a reproducible AST parser to extract and evaluate inline citations from LLM-generated Markdown reports at scale. Unlike methods that verify claims in isolation, our framework closes the loop by retrieving the actual cited content, enabling human or model evaluators to judge each citation against its source. Citations are evaluated along three dimensions. (1) Link Works verifies URL accessibility, (2) Relevant Content measures topical alignment, and (3) Fact Check validates factual accuracy against source content. We benchmark 14 closed-source and open-source LLMs across three evaluation dimensions using rubric-based LLM-as-a-judge evaluators calibrated through human review. Our results reveal that even the strongest frontier models maintain link validity above 94% and relevance above 80%, yet achieve only 39-77% factual accuracy, while fewer than half of open-source models successfully generate cited reports in a one-shot setting. Ablation studies on research depth show that Fact Check accuracy drops by approximately 42% on average across two frontier models as tool calls scale from 2 to 150, demonstrating that more retrieval does not produce more accurate citations. These findings reveal a critical disconnect between surface-level citation quality and factual reliability, and our framework provides the evaluation infrastructure to assess the disconnect.

</details>


### [146] [StraTA: Incentivizing Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Strategic Trajectory Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06642)
*Xiangyuan Xue,Yifan Zhou,Zidong Wang,Shengji Tang,Philip Torr,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出战略轨迹抽象（StraTA）框架，通过引入显式的轨迹级策略，结合分层GRPO风格的 rollout 设计、多样化策略 rollout 和关键自我评判，在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 SciWorld 等长 horizon 决策任务中显著提升了样本效率和最终性能，分别达到 93.1%、84.2% 和 63.5% 的成功率，超越强基线甚至前沿闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体在长 horizon 决策中主要采用纯反应式方法，导致探索和信用分配在长轨迹上效果不佳，亟需引入显式的轨迹级策略来改善决策质量。

Method: StraTA 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从初始任务状态采样紧凑策略，后续动作以此策略为条件；(2) 采用分层 GRPO 风格的 rollout 设计联合训练策略生成和动作执行；(3) 通过多样化策略 rollout 和关键自我评判机制增强探索和优化。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 上达到 93.1% 成功率，WebShop 上达到 84.2% 成功率，SciWorld 上取得 63.5% 总体分数，超越强基线和前沿闭源模型，同时样本效率也得到一致提升。

Conclusion: StraTA 通过显式引入轨迹级策略有效解决了长 horizon 决策中探索和信用分配的难题，验证了分层强化学习框架在 LLM 智能体优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interactive agents, but optimizing them for long-horizon decision making remains difficult because current methods are largely purely reactive, which weakens both exploration and credit assignment over extended trajectories. In this work, we present Strategic Trajectory Abstraction (StraTA), a simple framework that introduces an explicit trajectory-level strategy into agentic reinforcement learning (RL). StraTA samples a compact strategy from the initial task state, conditions subsequent actions on that strategy, and trains strategy generation and action execution jointly with a hierarchical GRPO-style rollout design, further enhanced by diverse strategy rollout and critical self-judgment. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld show that StraTA consistently improves both sample efficiency and final performance over strong baselines. StraTA reaches success rates of 93.1% on ALFWorld and 84.2% on WebShop. On SciWorld, StraTA attains a 63.5% overall score, outperforming frontier closed-source models.

</details>


### [147] [EMO: Pretraining Mixture of Experts for Emergent Modularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06663)
*Ryan Wang,Akshita Bhagia,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EMO通过文档级专家共享约束，在预训练中自发形成领域相关的专家分组，使得MoE模型可以仅使用部分专家子集进行推理而性能损失极小，实现了大型稀疏模型的模块化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型通常以单体系统部署，即使应用只需要代码、数学或特定领域知识等狭窄能力子集，也必须加载完整模型。MoE虽理论上能激活部分专家，但实践中限制专家子集会导致严重性能下降，限制了其在内存受限场景下的实用性。

Method: 提出EMO，核心思想是鼓励来自相似领域的token依赖相似的专家。由于同一文档内的token通常共享领域，EMO限制它们从共享的专家池中选择专家，而不同文档可使用不同专家池。这种简单约束利用文档边界在预训练中自发形成一致的专家分组。

Result: 预训练1B激活/14B总参数EMO模型：作为完整模型匹配标准MoE性能；仅保留25%（12.5%）的专家时，绝对性能仅下降1%（3%），而标准MoE在此设置下完全失效。专家子集形成语义级别（如数学、代码领域）的专门化，而非标准MoE的低级句法专门化。

Conclusion: EMO展示了大型稀疏模型模块化、内存高效部署的可行路径，为可组合架构开辟了新机会。

Abstract: Large language models are typically deployed as monolithic systems, requiring the full model even when applications need only a narrow subset of capabilities, e.g., code, math, or domain-specific knowledge. Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) seemingly offer a potential alternative by activating only a subset of experts per input, but in practice, restricting inference to a subset of experts for a given domain leads to severe performance degradation. This limits their practicality in memory-constrained settings, especially as models grow larger and sparser. We introduce EMO, an MoE designed for modularity-the independent use and composition of expert subsets-without requiring human-defined priors. Our key idea is to encourage tokens from similar domains to rely on similar experts. Since tokens within a document often share a domain, EMO restricts them to select experts from a shared pool, while allowing different documents to use different pools. This simple constraint enables coherent expert groupings to emerge during pretraining using document boundaries alone. We pretrain a 1B-active, 14B-total EMO on 1T tokens. As a full model, it matches standard MoE performance. Crucially, it enables selective expert use: retaining only 25% (12.5%) of experts incurs just a 1% (3%) absolute drop, whereas standard MoEs break under the same setting. We further find that expert subsets in EMO specialize at semantic levels (e.g., domains such as math or code), in contrast to the low-level syntactic specialization observed in standard MoEs. Altogether, our results demonstrate a path toward modular, memory-efficient deployment of large, sparse models and open new opportunities for composable architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [A GPU-Accelerated Hybrid Method for a Class of Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05208)
*Zhenyu Lei,Jin-Kao Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成学习驱动交叉算子、多惩罚可行-不可行搜索框架和GPU加速的混合算法，用于高效求解多车场车辆路径问题，在三个变体的大规模算例上达到最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDVRP在实际中广泛应用，但其固有复杂性导致计算求解极具挑战性，现有方法在效率和可扩展性方面仍有不足。

Method: (1) 学习驱动的多样性控制路径交换交叉算子；(2) 多惩罚评价函数指导的多车场可行-不可行搜索框架；(3) 两个专用车场相关局部搜索算子；(4) 基于张量的GPU加速结合新型多移动更新策略。

Result: 在三个MDVRP变体的基准算例上，所提算法与最先进方法相比极具竞争力，尤其在大规模算例上表现突出。

Conclusion: 该混合算法有效解决了MDVRP的计算难题，GPU加速版本显著提升了可扩展性，为大规模多车场路径优化提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Multi-depot vehicle routing problems (MDVRPs) are prevalent in a variety of practical applications. However, they are computationally challenging to solve due to their inherent complexity. This paper proposes an effective hybrid algorithm for a class of MDVRPs. The algorithm integrates a learning-driven, diversity-controlled route-exchange crossover and a multi-depot-supported feasible-and-infeasible search framework guided by a multi-penalty evaluation function. Two dedicated depot-related local search operators are incorporated to further strengthen the search capability in multi-depot settings. To improve computational efficiency and scalability, an enhanced version of the algorithm is developed that uses a tensor-based GPU acceleration combined with a novel multi-move update strategy. Extensive computational experiments on benchmark instances of three MDVRP variants show that the proposed algorithms are highly competitive with state-of-the-art methods, especially for large-scale instances.

</details>


### [149] [DexSim2Real: Foundation Model-Guided Sim-to-Real Transfer for Generalizable Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05241)
*Zijian Zeng,Fei Ding,Huiming Yang,Xianwei Li,Yuhao Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSim2Real是一个利用视觉-语言基础模型弥合灵巧操作模拟到现实迁移鸿沟的集成框架，通过基础模型引导的域随机化、触觉-视觉交叉注意力策略和渐进式技能课程三组件，在六项任务中实现78.2%的真实世界成功率，将模拟到现实性能差距缩小至仅8.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟到现实的迁移仍然是灵巧操作策略部署的关键瓶颈，现有方法依赖手动设计的域随机化或任务特定适应，缺乏跨多样化操作场景的泛化能力。

Method: 提出DexSim2Real框架，包含三个组件：(1) FM-DR：利用视觉-语言模型作为视觉真实性评判器，通过闭环CMA-ES优化仿真参数；(2) TVCAP：采用交叉注意力机制融合视觉与触觉信息，实现零样本模拟到现实强化学习；(3) PSC：基于大语言模型的任务分解与难度调度器，针对接触密集型灵巧任务设计渐进式技能课程。

Result: 在六项挑战性灵巧操作任务的盲评估中，DexSim2Real达到78.2%的平均真实世界成功率，优于DrEureka和DeXtreme，模拟到现实性能差距仅8.3%。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言基础模型能有效弥合灵巧操作的模拟到现实鸿沟，视觉反馈补充了纯文本方法的不足，触觉-视觉融合与渐进式课程学习进一步提升了策略的迁移鲁棒性。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer remains a critical bottleneck for deploying dexterous manipulation policies learned in simulation to real-world robots. Existing approaches rely on manually designed domain randomization or task-specific adaptation, limiting their generalizability across diverse manipulation scenarios. We present DexSim2Real, an integrated framework that leverages vision-language foundation models to bridge the sim-to-real gap for dexterous manipulation. Our system combines three components: (1) Foundation Model-Guided Domain Randomization (FM-DR), which uses a vision-language model as a visual realism critic to optimize simulation parameters via closed-loop CMA-ES, complementing text-based approaches like DrEureka with direct visual feedback; (2) a Tactile-Visual Cross-Attention Policy (TVCAP) that adapts cross-attention visuo-tactile fusion to zero-shot sim-to-real RL; and (3) a Progressive Skill Curriculum (PSC) that builds on LLM-based task decomposition with a difficulty scheduler tailored to contact-rich dexterous tasks. Extensive experiments on six challenging manipulation tasks with blinded evaluation demonstrate that DexSim2Real achieves a 78.2% average real-world success rate, outperforming DrEureka and DeXtreme while reducing the sim-to-real performance gap to only 8.3%.

</details>


### [150] [Track A*: Fast Visibility-Aware Trajectory Planning for Active Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05338)
*Hanxuan Chen,Kangli Wang,Ji Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TA*是一种离线搜索轨迹规划器，在四维时空网格上针对可见性感知目标跟踪进行优化，通过分层DAG搜索、BVH碰撞缓存、波束剪枝和多射线可见性评估器，在牺牲理论最优性的条件下实现了近乎基线的跟踪可见性，同时将规划速度提升23倍，收敛率从56.9%提升至100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线参考轨迹对于构建多模态目标跟踪数据集和在可重复条件下基准测试在线跟踪规划器至关重要，但现有方法在效率和可扩展性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出TA*算法，在四维时空网格(x,y,z,t)上采用分层有向无环图(DAG)搜索，结合三种工程优化：(1) 基于包围体层次结构(BVH)的跨时间障碍物距离缓存；(2) 每层波束剪枝；(3) 可配置多射线可见性评估器，通过波束剪枝启发式搜索高效寻找高质量跟踪轨迹。

Result: 在1000场景压力测试中全部收敛，耗时45秒(32 workers)；与未优化的优先队列A*基线相比，平均规划时间降低23.0倍，最坏情况降低11.8倍，收敛率从56.9%提升至100%；在基线收敛的子集上，平均可见性仅下降0.15个百分点，无场景超过5个百分点下降。

Conclusion: TA*可作为特定条件下实用的离线参考规划器，但在Town07密集植被等环境中存在局限性和失败案例。

Abstract: Offline reference trajectories for active target tracking are needed both for building multi-modal tracking datasets and for benchmarking online tracking planners under repeatable conditions. We present Track A star (TA star), an offline search-based trajectory planner that targets the visibility-aware target tracking objective on a discretized four-dimensional spatio-temporal grid (x, y, z, t). TA star combines a layered Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) search with three engineering optimizations: cross-time obstacle distance caching against a Bounding Volume Hierarchy (BVH), per-layer beam pruning, and a configurable multi-ray visibility evaluator. TA star employs a beam-pruned heuristic search on this discrete graph to efficiently find high-quality tracking trajectories. While it trades strict theoretical optimality for practical scalability, our empirical results demonstrate robust, near-baseline visibility performance at a fraction of the computational cost. On a 1000-scenario stress test across eight CARLA Optimized maps, TA star converges on all scenarios and completes in 45 s using 32 workers; on a 248-scenario controlled comparison against an unoptimized priority-queue A star baseline (BinaryHeap implementation) under identical scenario inputs and a 5 x 10^6 expansion cap, TA star reduces mean planning time by 23.0x and worst-case planning time by 11.8x, while raising convergence from 56.9% to 100%. On the n=141 baseline-converged subset, TA star changes average visibility by only -0.15 percentage points (pp), with no scenario exceeding a 5 pp drop. We position TA star as a practical offline reference planner under these specific conditions, with limitations and failure cases discussed for environments such as Town07 dense vegetation.

</details>


### [151] [Passive Fault Tolerance through Tension-to-Thrust Feed-Forward: Hybrid Input-to-State Stability for Decentralized Multi-UAV Slung-Load Transport under Abrupt Cable Severance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05339)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Paul Schmitt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种被动式架构，将每架无人机的缆绳张力直接馈入高度推力指令，并结合PD、防摆和投影级联控制器，实现了多无人机吊挂运输系统中缆绳突发断裂后的分散式无通信恢复，并通过混合实用输入状态稳定性（hybrid practical ISS）证书证明了恢复包络。在五架无人机、10 kg载荷的Drake多体仿真中，单双断裂场景下RMSE为0.312-0.328 m，峰值下垂76.1-95.2 mm，恢复时间在一个载荷摆周期内。禁用张力前馈后巡航误差增加34-39%，峰值下垂增大3.6-4.0倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机吊挂运输中缆绳突发断裂会重新分配负载并改变主动约束集，留给故障诊断和重构的时间极短。现有控制器依赖协调力分配、同伴状态交换或固定缆绳拓扑，缺乏针对突发断裂的认证分散式恢复机制。

Method: 提出被动式架构，将各无人机测量的缆绳张力直接作为高度推力前馈指令(T_i^ff=T_i)，配合PD-防摆-投影级联控制器实现无通信分散控制。核心贡献是条件混合实用输入状态稳定性证书，将松弛偏移受限的紧缆简化、断裂后Lyapunov有界跳跃、故障间衰减和每故障周期收缩(ρ∈(0,1))组合成显式恢复包络。

Result: 在五架无人机、10 kg载荷的Drake多体仿真中，采用Kelvin-Voigt缆绳和Dryden风模型，单双断裂场景下闭环RMSE为0.312-0.328 m，峰值下垂76.1-95.2 mm，恢复时间在一个载荷摆周期内。禁用张力前馈后巡航误差增加34-39%，峰值下垂增大3.6-4.0倍。

Conclusion: 局部张力前馈是被测试分散式级联中的主导被动恢复机制，所提出的架构能在无通信条件下实现缆绳突发断裂后的认证恢复。

Abstract: Abrupt cable severance in multi-UAV slung-load transport redistributes load and changes the active constraint set, leaving limited time for fault diagnosis and reconfiguration. Existing controllers rely on coordinated force allocation, peer-state exchange, or fixed cable topology, and therefore lack a certified decentralized recovery mechanism for unannounced severance. We present a passive architecture that routes each vehicle's measured cable tension directly into its altitude thrust command, $T_i^{\mathrm{ff}}=T_i$, while a surrounding proportional-derivative, anti-swing, and projection cascade preserves local tracking feasibility. The main contribution is a conditional hybrid practical input-to-state-stability certificate that composes a slack-excursion-bounded taut-cable reduction, bounded post-severance Lyapunov jumps, inter-fault decay, and per-fault-cycle contraction $ρ\in (0,1)$ into an explicit recovery envelope under stated actuator, slack, and dwell assumptions. We validate the controller in Drake multibody simulation with five vehicles, a 10 kg payload, Kelvin-Voigt cables, Dryden wind, and single- and dual-severance schedules: the closed loop attains 0.312-0.328 m RMSE, 76.1-95.2 mm peak sag, and recovery within one payload-pendulum period. Disabling the identity inflates cruise error by 34-39% and peak sag by 3.6x-4.0x, identifying local tension feed-forward as the dominant passive recovery mechanism in the tested decentralized cascade.

</details>


### [152] [Creative Robot Tool Use by Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05411)
*M. Tuluhan Akbulut,Varun Satheesh,Ahmed Jaafar,Alper Ahmetoglu,Shane Parr,Aditya Ganeshan,Shivam Vats,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于因果推理的机器人创造性工具使用框架，通过在动力学模型中进行模拟实验发现工具与任务间的因果关系，结合VLM特征建议和反事实工具生成，实现基于因果特征的工具选择与技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人工具使用研究往往局限于工具的主要功能，缺乏让机器人识别工具超越其原始用途的创造性使用能力。需要一种能够发现工具与任务间因果关系的框架，使机器人能够泛化地选择和使用非标准工具。

Method: 将因果发现问题解耦为两个互补组件：(1) VLM（视觉语言模型）特征建议；(2) 通过定向几何和物理特征扰动生成反事实工具。然后基于识别的因果特征对新物体进行分类，并通过基于因果特征的关键点匹配迁移工具使用技能。整个流程在动力学模型中重建任务，将工具使用锚定在问题的物理规律上。

Result: 在三个任务场景（用不同棍子够到远处物体、用多样物品从碗中舀糖果、用不同箱子或板条箱作为台阶取高处物品）中验证了方法有效性。基线对比表明，识别因果特征并将其锚定在物理工具属性中能实现更可靠的工具选择和更强的技能关键点迁移。

Conclusion: 将因果发现与物理仿真结合是提升机器人创造性工具使用能力的关键方向，因果特征的识别和物理 grounding 对于工具选择的可靠性和技能迁移的鲁棒性至关重要。

Abstract: We propose a causal reasoning framework for creative robot tool use where a suitable tool for a task is correctly identified for use beyond its primary objectives. The proposed framework first discovers the causal relationships between the tool and the task by conducting simulated experiments in a dynamics model. We decouple the causal discovery problem into two complementary components: VLM-based feature suggestion and counterfactual tool generation via targeted geometric and physical feature perturbations. Then, novel objects are classified based on identified causal features, and the tool use skill is transferred via keypoint matching conditioned on the identified causal features. By reconstructing the task in a dynamics model, our approach grounds tool use in the physics of the problem. We illustrate our approach in reaching a distant object with different sticks, scooping candies from a bowl using diverse items, and using different boxes or crates as stepping platforms to retrieve an object from a high shelf. Our baseline comparisons show that identifying causal features and grounding them in physical tool properties leads to more reliable tool selection and stronger skill keypoint transfer.

</details>


### [153] [Contact-Free Grasp Stability Prediction with In-Hand Time-of-Flight Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05461)
*Kyle DuFrene,Cindy Grimm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种使用多区域飞行时间传感器的非接触式抓取稳定性预测方法，无需抓取物体即可预测，在15Hz频率下实现85.5%-86.0%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取规划方法在高噪声传感器等条件下性能下降，而基于触觉的抓取稳定性分类器需要接触并抓取物体才能检测失败，效率较低。

Method: 在夹爪远端连杆安装多区域飞行时间传感器，收集2500+次真实世界抓取数据训练分类器，在15Hz频率下进行无接触抓取稳定性预测。

Result: 验证集准确率85.5%，测试集准确率86.0%，在15个训练物体和3个未见测试物体上均表现良好。

Conclusion: 提出的非接触式方法能显著加快稳定性分类速度，在不抓取物体的情况下实现高精度预测，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Current approaches to grasp planning for robotics demonstrate high success rates, but degrade with noisy sensors and other factors. Previous works have proposed tactile-based grasp stability classifiers to detect failures, but these approaches rely on making contact and grasping the object to do so. We propose a contact-free grasp stability predictor using multi-zone time-of-flight sensors mounted in the distal links of a gripper. Our method, as it does not require grasping the object to make a prediction, significantly speeds up the stability classification process, cycling at 15 Hz. We collected over 2,500 real-world grasps across 15 objects to train a classifier. Additionally, we conducted grasp attempts over six additional unseen objects, three for validation and model selection, and three for model testing. Our approach demonstrated strong classification performance, with an accuracy of 85.5% on validation and 86.0% on test objects.

</details>


### [154] [Robust $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ Controller Design For INDI-Controlled Quadrotor Using Online Parameter Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05483)
*Tom Aantjes,Till M. Blaha,Spilios Theodoulis,Ewoud J. J. Smeur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于增量非线性动态反演(INDI)的四旋翼姿态控制器，提出了一种结合在线参数辨识的鲁棒增益调度控制方法。通过信号基H∞闭环整形为对称四旋翼设计了带前馈滤波器的级联增益调度控制器，在非线性仿真和实际飞行测试中均展现出良好的稳定裕度和跟踪性能，即使辨识参数远超不确定范围，在作动器时间常数低于40ms时仍能维持可接受的飞行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有INDI控制器虽能在半秒内完成全部物理参数的在线估计（足够在空中抛掷过程中完成辨识），但尚缺乏一种根据模型参数鲁棒地调节外环增益的方法。本文旨在填补这一空白，设计一种依赖于模型参数的鲁棒增益调度控制器。

Method: 采用基于INDI的内环结合在线系统参数辨识，针对对称四旋翼使用信号基H∞闭环整形方法，合成带前馈滤波器的增益调度级联姿态控制器。

Result: 控制器具有良好的稳定裕度；非线性仿真验证了不确定性下的有效跟踪性能；飞行测试中即使辨识参数远超预设不确定范围，在作动器时间常数低于40ms时仍能维持接近仿真结果的可接受飞行性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒增益调度INDI姿态控制器能有效结合在线参数辨识，在参数极端偏差情况下仍保持良好性能，为四旋翼在全自动参数辨识后的鲁棒控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: It has recently been shown that all physical parameters of an Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) controller can be estimated onboard a multirotor within half a second, which is fast enough to do the full identification during a throw in the air. However, a robust method to tune outer loop gains for this feedback-linearizing INDI controller depending on the model parameters is still missing. This work presents the design of a robust gain-scheduled controller for attitude control of quadrotor, using an INDI-based inner loop with online identification of its system parameters. A gain-scheduled cascaded attitude controller with a feedforward filter is synthesized for a symmetric quadrotor using signal-based $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ closed-loop shaping. The resulting controller exhibits good stability margins, with nonlinear simulations confirming effective tracking performance under uncertainty. Experimental evaluation is also conducted through flight tests with full online parameter identification. Even though the identified parameters during these tests are far outside the defined uncertainty range, acceptable flight performance comparable to simulation results is maintained for actuator time constants below 40 ms.

</details>


### [155] [Real-world Latency Analysis of Vehicular Visible Light Communication with Multiple LED Transmitters and an Event-Based Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05541)
*Ryota Soga,Tsukasa Shimizu,Shintaro Shiba,Quan Kong,Shan Lu,Takaya Yamazato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件相机的可见光通信系统，通过正事件模式、协议设计和多发射器识别方法，解决了带宽饱和、多发射器接收和延迟表征三大挑战，并在真实车辆场景中验证了其满足协同感知需求，证明事件相机VLC可作为现有V2X技术的可行补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率、低延迟和宽动态范围等优势，使其成为车载可见光通信（VLC）中极具前景的接收器。然而，现有系统面临带宽饱和、多发射器同时接收困难和延迟表征不足等挑战，需要设计专门的解决方案来发挥事件相机在V2X应用中的潜力。

Method: 本文采用正事件模式（仅检测亮度增加的事件），设计了一种在抑制事件生成的同时保持通信距离和宽视野的协议；提出了一种识别多个发射器的方法，实现了最多三路LED的同时接收；并在真实车辆场景中评估了端到端延迟。

Result: 系统成功实现了三路LED的同时接收，并在真实车辆场景中验证了端到端延迟满足协同感知的要求。

Conclusion: 基于事件相机的VLC系统可作为现有V2X技术（如射频通信）的可行补充方案。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution, low latency, and wide dynamic range, making them promising receivers for visible light communication (VLC) in vehicle-to-everything (V2X) applications. This work presents an event-camera-based VLC system addressing three key challenges: bandwidth saturation, multi-transmitter reception, and latency characterization.
  We adopt a positive-event-only mode and design a protocol that suppresses event generation while maintaining communication distance and a wide field of view. We also propose a method to identify multiple transmitters and demonstrate simultaneous reception from up to three LEDs. Finally, we evaluate end-to-end latency in real vehicular scenarios and show that the system meets cooperative perception requirements. These results demonstrate that event-camera-based VLC is a feasible complement to existing V2X technologies (e.g., RF).

</details>


### [156] [On the Emergence of Pendular Structure in Multi-Contact Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05707)
*Lingxue Lyu,Zihui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了线性倒立摆模型（LIPM）在腿式运动控制中的最优性来源，通过质心最优控制问题发现：全秩支撑时最优力模式趋近于摆线模式（由力矩雅可比SVD决定，足部几何误差<16%）；双足支撑（如trot）时摩擦锥对角动量变化率存在下界且存在非光滑可行性拐点；加入非零角动量变化率任务项会可预测地偏离摆线集。实验在点质量四足机器人和Unitree Go1上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIPM在腿式运动控制中广泛使用，但通常是作为建模选择而非控制器代价函数的实际偏好结果。本文旨在明确建立LIPM与优化控制代价之间的理论联系。

Method: 从一个小型质心最优控制问题（OCP）出发，惩罚角动量变化率，分析最优解的结构特性。通过SVD分解力矩雅可比分析全秩支撑情况，对双足支撑考虑摩擦锥约束，推导可行性拐点闭式解。在点质量四足机器人和Unitree Go1（MuJoCo仿真）上通过开环QP和闭环节矩级控制器验证。

Result: 1) 全秩支撑时最优力模式向摆线模式漂移，速率由力矩雅可比SVD决定，常数由足部几何设置决定（实验误差<16%）；2) 双足支撑（trot）时摩擦锥引入角动量变化率的下界，权重调节无法消除；3) 存在非光滑可行性拐点，对应临界水平加速度有闭式解；4) 加入非零角动量变化率任务项会可预测地偏离摆线集。

Conclusion: LIPM的最优性可以从角动量变化率惩罚的最优控制问题中推导出来，与经典ZMP/DCM图像一致。但闭环控制下渐近分析会偏离实际行为，需要谨慎使用。

Abstract: LIPM is everywhere in legged-locomotion control, but almost always as a modeling choice rather than as something the
  controller's cost actually prefers. This note tries to make that link more explicit. Working from a small centroidal
  OCP that penalizes the rate of angular momentum, we look at what its optimum tends to look like. Three things come
  out. With full-rank stance, the optimum drifts toward a pendular force pattern at a rate determined by the SVD of the
  moment Jacobian; the constant is set by foot-span geometry and matches the experiments to within 16%. With N=2 stance,
  as in trot, the friction cone introduces a lower bound on $\|\dot{H}_G\|$ that no amount of weight tuning fixes; we
  also see a non-smooth feasibility kink at a critical horizontal acceleration that we can write in closed form. Adding
  a task term that asks for a nonzero $\dot{H}_G$ moves the optimum off the pendular set in a predictable way. None of
  this is far from the classical ZMP/DCM picture. We test these claims on a point-mass quadruped and on the Unitree Go1
  in MuJoCo (open-loop QP and a torque-level closed-loop controller), and we note where the asymptotic story stops being
  a good description of what the closed loop actually does.

</details>


### [157] [MaMi-HOI: Harmonizing Global Kinematics and Local Geometry for Human-Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05756)
*Hao Wang,Shiqi Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MaMi-HOI框架，通过几何感知邻近适配器(GAPA)和运动和谐适配器(KHA)解决扩散模型中几何遗忘问题，在保持自然运动的同时实现精确的人-物交互接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在语义对齐上表现优异，但难以维持精确的物体接触。研究发现扩散模型深度增加时，语义特征会掩盖物体几何特征，导致模型丧失对物体几何的感知能力，即几何遗忘问题。

Method: 提出MaMi-HOI分层框架：1) 几何感知邻近适配器(GAPA)显式注入密集物体细节进行残差咬合校正；2) 运动和谐适配器(KHA)主动对齐全身姿态与空间目标，确保骨架适应约束的同时保持自然性。

Result: 实验验证MaMi-HOI能同时实现自然运动与精确接触，并扩展生成长时任务和复杂轨迹，弥合全局导航与高保真操作之间的差距。

Conclusion: MaMi-HOI有效协调了宏观运动流畅性与微观空间精度，解决了3D人-物交互生成中语义与物理约束的矛盾。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D Human-Object Interactions (HOI) is a fundamental task for applications ranging from embodied AI to virtual content creation, which requires harmonizing high-level semantic intent with strict low-level physical constraints. Existing methods excel at semantic alignment, however, they struggle to maintain precise object contact. We reveal a key finding termed \textit{Geometric Forgetting}: as diffusion model depth increases, semantic feature tend to overshadow object geometry feature, causing the model to lose its perception to object geometry. To address this, we propose MaMi-HOI, a hierarchical framework reconciling \textbf{Ma}cro-level kinematic fluidity with \textbf{Mi}cro-level spatial precision. First, to counteract geometric forgetting, we introduce the Geometry-Aware Proximity Adapter (GAPA), which explicitly re-injects dense object details to perform residual snapping corrections for precise contact. Nevertheless, such aggressive local enforcement can disrupt global dynamics, leading to robotic stiffness. In response, we introduce the Kinematic Harmony Adapter (KHA), which proactively aligns whole-body posture with spatial objectives, ensuring the skeleton actively accommodates constraints without compromising naturalness. Extensive experiments validate that MaMi-HOI simultaneously achieves natural motion and precise contact. Crucially, it extends generation capabilities to long-term tasks with complex trajectories, effectively bridging the gap between global navigation and high-fidelity manipulation in 3D scenes. Code is available at https://github.com/DON738110198/MaMi-HOI.git

</details>


### [158] [A Comparative Study of INDI and NDI with Nonlinear Disturbance Observer for Aerial Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05825)
*Benedetta Rota,Mirko Mizzoni,Amr Afifi,Pieter van Goor,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过仿真对比了增量非线性动态逆（INDI）和带非线性扰动观测器的非线性动态逆（NDI+NDO）在全驱动空中机器人中的鲁棒性，结果表明INDI在多种模型失配和组合应力场景下鲁棒性更强，而NDI+NDO在标称性能上相当但在非理想条件下更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估和比较INDI与NDI+NDO两种非线性控制策略在全驱动空中机器人中的鲁棒性表现，为实际应用提供选型指导。

Method: 采用基于仿真的对比分析，在代表性运行场景下系统性地比较两种方法在参数变化、外部扰动和测量噪声下的跟踪性能、鲁棒性和控制能耗。

Result: INDI在多种模型失配和组合应力场景下表现出更强的鲁棒性；NDI+NDO在标称条件下性能与INDI相当，但在多种非理想条件下敏感性更高。

Conclusion: 研究为增量式与基于观测器的反演控制策略在全驱动空中机器人应用中的相对优势和局限性提供了实用指导。

Abstract: This work presents a simulation-based comparative robustness analysis of Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) and Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion augmented with a nonlinear disturbance observer (NDI+NDO) for fully actuated aerial robots. A systematic simulation campaign across representative operating scenarios is conducted, where we compare tracking performance, robustness, control effort, under parametric variations, external disturbances, and measurement noise. Results show that INDI demonstrates stronger robustness in several model-mismatch and combined-stress cases, while NDI+NDO primarily matches nominal performance but exhibits greater sensitivity under several non-ideal conditions. These findings provide practical guidance on the relative strengths and limitations of incremental and observer-based inversion strategies for aerial robotic applications.

</details>


### [159] [Cycle-resolved Cephalopod-Inspired Pulsed-Jet Robot With High-Volume Expulsion and Drag-Reduced Gliding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05875)
*Yiyuan Zhang,Anye Zhong,Junkai Chen,Wenci Xin,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文受头足类动物脉冲喷射推进的循环周期启发，提出了一种采用刚柔混合折纸结构的仿生脉冲喷射机器人。该机器人的"外套膜"通过刚性折叠面板与柔性硅胶框架集成，在喷射阶段可实现75%的有效腔体体积缩减，并在收缩滑翔构型下将投影截面积阻力降低约75.7%。实验表明，机器人在首个喷射周期内达到约0.5 m/s（3.8 BL/s）的峰值速度和超过0.2 m/s（1.5 BL/s）的平均速度。文章通过周期分解框架分别研究了喷射体积、滑翔时长和填充路径对整体循环运动性能的影响，并验证了高喷射体积比收缩、减阻滑翔以及被动进气阀辅助填充的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头足类动物的脉冲喷射推进并非孤立的喷射事件，而是包含喷射、被动滑翔和外套膜填充的协调循环周期。现有机器人往往仅关注单次喷射，缺乏对完整循环动力学的研究。本文旨在提出一种能够实现主动驱动力与几何引导变形相结合的仿生机器人平台，以系统研究喷射-滑翔-填充循环中各环节对整体运动性能的影响。

Method: 设计了一种刚柔混合折纸外套膜结构，将刚性折叠面板嵌入柔性硅胶框架中，实现大变形量的主动驱动与几何引导变形。构建了周期分解的实验框架，分别独立研究喷射体积、滑翔时长和填充路径三个子环节对整周期运动性能的影响。在机器人上集成了外套膜孔启发的被动进气阀以辅助填充。

Result: 机器人在首个喷射周期内峰值速度约0.5 m/s（3.8 BL/s），平均速度超过0.2 m/s（1.5 BL/s）。外套膜可实现75%的有效腔体体积缩减；收缩滑翔构型下投影截面积阻力降低约75.7%。实验验证了高喷射体积比收缩对速度生成的关键作用、不同滑翔时长下的减阻效果，以及被动进气阀对填充的辅助作用。

Conclusion: 本文不仅提供了一种主动可变形头足类仿生喷射推进的机器人实现方案，还建立了一个统一的实验平台，用于研究脉冲喷射推进中喷射-滑翔-填充的完整循环动力学，为后续仿生水下机器人的设计和控制奠定了基础。

Abstract: Cephalopod pulsed-jet locomotion is not a single isolated expulsion event, but a coordinated cycle involving jet expulsion, passive gliding, and mantle refilling. Inspired by this cycle-resolved biological strategy, this paper presents a cephalopod-inspired pulsed-jet robot with a rigid-soft hybrid origami mantle that enables large, actively driven, and geometry-guided body deformation. The proposed mantle integrates rigid folding panels with a compliant silicone framework, allowing a 75% effective cavity-volume reduction during expulsion and reducing the projected cross-sectional drag area by approximately 75.7% in the contracted gliding configuration. Using this platform, we formulate a cycle-resolved framework to separately investigate how expelled volume, glide duration, and refill pathway influence whole-cycle locomotion performance. Experiments show that the robot reaches a peak speed of approximately 0.5 m/s (3.8 BL/s) and an average speed exceeding 0.2 m/s (1.5 BL/s) within the first jetting cycle. The results further demonstrate the roles of high expelled-volume-ratio contraction in speed generation, reduced-drag-area gliding under different glide durations, and mantle-aperture-inspired passive inlet valves in assisting refill. This work provides both a robotic implementation of actively deformable cephalopod-like jet propulsion and a unified experimental platform for studying expulsion-gliding-refilling dynamics in pulsed-jet locomotion.

</details>


### [160] [Generating Roadside LiDAR Datasets from Vehicle-Side Datasets via Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05897)
*Yuhan Xia,Runxin Zhao,Hanyang Zhuang,Chunxiang Wang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VRS框架，通过车辆端LiDAR数据合成带标注的路侧数据集，利用点云补全和占用率可见性约束解决车-路域差异，实验证明合成数据能有效增强路侧3D目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧感知扩展了ITS的感知覆盖范围，但大规模标注路侧LiDAR数据集稀缺，限制了高性能路侧感知模型的训练。

Method: 提出VRS框架：从车辆端数据集通过LiDAR新视角合成生成带标签路侧数据；采用车辆点云补全补偿缺失几何信息；引入基于占用率的可见性约束处理跨视角渲染中的大视角变化。

Result: 合成数据有效补充真实路侧数据，缓解真实数据有限的问题，并提升对未见路侧视角的泛化能力。

Conclusion: VRS框架能够从车辆端数据灵活合成多视角路侧数据集，为缓解路侧数据稀缺问题提供有效方案。

Abstract: Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) require reliable environmental perception to support safe and efficient transportation. With the rapid development of Vehicle-to-everything (V2X), roadside perception has become an effective means to extend sensing coverage and improve traffic safety. However, the scarcity of large-scale annotated roadside LiDAR datasets poses a major challenge for training high-performance roadside perception models. In this paper, we introduce Vehicle-to-Roadside LiDAR Synthesis (VRS), a data synthesis framework that generates labeled roadside LiDAR datasets from vehicle-side datasets via LiDAR novel view synthesis. To mitigate the vehicle-to-roadside domain gap, VRS employs vehicle point cloud completion to compensate for missing geometry in vehicle-side observations, and introduces an occupancy-based visibility constraint to handle large viewpoint changes during cross-view rendering. The proposed framework enables flexible multi-view rendering for scalable roadside data generation. Extensive experiments on roadside 3D object detection demonstrate that the synthesized data effectively complements real roadside data, mitigates the limitations of limited real-world roadside data, and improves generalization to unseen roadside viewpoints.

</details>


### [161] [DexSynRefine: Synthesizing and Refining Human-Object Interaction Motion for Physically Feasible Dexterous Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05925)
*Hyesung Lee,Hyunwoo Jung,Si-Hwan Heo,Sungwook Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSynRefine是一个结合运动流形基元合成、任务空间残差强化学习和接触动力学自适应的框架，从稀疏的人-物交互演示数据中学习灵巧操作技能，在五个任务上比运动学重定向方法提升50-70个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人-物交互数据学习灵巧操作可替代遥操作，但演示数据稀疏且仅提供运动学信息，无法直接应对具身差异和接触丰富的动力学问题。

Method: 提出DexSynRefine框架，包含三个组件：1) HOI-MMFP，扩展运动流形基元以合成协调的手-物轨迹；2) 任务空间残差强化学习策略，在保持运动学结构的同时物理接地合成参考轨迹；3) 接触与动力学自适应模块，利用本体感受历史实现仿真到真实的迁移。

Result: 在五个灵巧操作任务（抓取放置、工具使用、物体重定向）上，任务空间残差策略在仿真中优于先前的动作表征基线，并在真实机器人上完成全部五个任务，相比运动学重定向提升50-70个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了从稀疏HOI数据学习灵巧操作的具身差异和动力学接地问题，并实现了从仿真到真实机器人的成功迁移。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation from human-object interaction (HOI) data is a scalable alternative to teleoperation, but HOI demonstrations are sparse and provide only kinematic motion that is not directly executable under embodiment mismatch and contact-rich dynamics. We present DexSynRefine, a framework with three coupled components: HOI-MMFP, a task- and object-initial-state-conditioned extension of motion manifold primitives that synthesizes coordinated hand-object trajectories from sparse HOI demonstrations; a task-space residual RL policy that physically grounds the synthesized reference while inheriting its kinematic structure; and a contact-and-dynamics adaptation module that enables sim-to-real transfer from proprioceptive history. Across five dexterous manipulation tasks spanning pick-and-place, tool use, and object reorientation, our task-space residual policy outperforms prior action-representation baselines in simulations and transfers to a real robot on all five tasks, improving over kinematic retargeting by 50-70 percentage points.

</details>


### [162] [Plug-and-Play Label Map Diffusion for Universal Goal-Oriented Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05960)
*Zhixuan Shen,Yijie Zeng,Shengxiang Luo,Tianrui Li,Haonan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PLMD，一种基于DDPM的即插即用地图补全扩散模型，通过生成未观测区域的障碍物和语义标签来增强部分观测环境下的目标定位能力，在三个GON任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于地图的GON方法通常使用自中心语义地图，面临对完整地图的依赖和语义关联不一致两大挑战，亟需一种能在部分观测环境下可靠定位未观测目标的方法。

Method: 提出PLMD模型，基于DDPM构建地图补全扩散模型，通过扩散过程生成未观测区域的障碍物标签和语义标签；利用已知与未知障碍物布局的结构一致性，将障碍物先验融入语义去噪过程以缓解语义关联不一致问题。

Result: PLMD有效扩展未知地图区域，能无缝集成到现有依赖语义地图的导航策略中，在三个GON任务上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: PLMD通过扩散模型实现了部分观测环境下的地图补全与目标定位，是一种即插即用的高效解决方案。

Abstract: In embodied vision, Goal-Oriented Navigation (GON) requires robots to locate a specific goal within an unexplored environment. The primary challenge of GON arises from the need to construct a Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) map to understand the environment while simultaneously localizing an unobserved goal. Existing map-based methods typically employ self-centered semantic maps, often facing challenges such as reliance on complete maps or inconsistent semantic association. To this end, we propose Plug-and-Play Label Map Diffusion (PLMD), which defines a novel map completion diffusion model based on Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM). PLMD generates obstacle and semantic labels for unobserved regions through a diffusion-based completion process, thereby enabling goal localization even in partially observed environments. Moreover, it mitigates inconsistent semantic association by leveraging structural consistency between known and unknown obstacle layouts and integrating obstacle priors into the semantic denoising process. By substituting predicted labels for unobserved regions, robots can accurately localize the specified objects. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PLMD \textbf{(I)} effectively expands the region of unknown maps, \textbf{(II)} integrates seamlessly into existing navigation strategies that rely on semantic maps, \textbf{(III)} achieves state-of-the-art performance on three GON tasks.

</details>


### [163] [Accurate Trajectory Tracking with MPCC for Flapping-Wing MAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06042)
*Charbel Toumieh,Jack Zeng,Niel Mistry,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型预测轮廓控制（MPCC）的扑翼飞行器自主控制方法，通过弧长参数化轨迹和在线优化进度，结合紧凑连续可微的耦合动力学模型，在XFly扑翼飞行器上实现了6.5-9厘米的轨迹跟踪误差，较此前方法提升近10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼微型飞行器相比旋翼无人机更安静安全，但其升力、空速和转向能力高度耦合且控制输入有限，传统级联控制器无法独立处理这些变量，导致复杂机动中存在持续跟踪误差；同时，时间参数化轨迹跟踪需要预定义速度曲线，现有方法无法鲁棒地应对耦合动力学。

Method: 采用模型预测轮廓控制（MPCC），跟踪弧长参数化轨迹并在线优化进度，无需预定义时序；提出一个紧凑、连续可微的动力学模型，捕捉鸟尺度扑翼飞行器的主要耦合效应，满足实时非线性优化的计算预算。

Result: 在XFly扑翼飞行器上沿圆形和三维竞速轨迹飞行，在最高3 m/s速度下，平均轨迹偏差为6.5-9厘米，较此前扑翼控制方法提升近10倍。

Conclusion: 所提MPCC方法结合紧凑耦合动力学模型，有效解决了扑翼飞行器自主控制中多变量耦合和时序依赖问题，实现了高精度轨迹跟踪，为鸟尺度扑翼飞行器的精确自主控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Flapping-wing micro aerial vehicles offer quieter and safer operation than rotary-wing drones, yet achieving precise autonomous control of bird-scale ornithopters remains challenging: lift, airspeed, and turning authority are tightly coupled and governed by only a few control inputs. Conventional cascaded controllers treat altitude, speed, and heading independently, producing persistent tracking errors during complex maneuvers, while time-parameterized trajectory tracking requires predefined speed profiles that existing methods cannot robustly produce for these coupled dynamics. We address both limitations simultaneously with a Model Predictive Contouring Control (MPCC) approach that tracks arc-length-parameterized trajectories while optimizing progress online, eliminating the need for predefined timing. However, MPCC requires a dynamical model that captures the coupled aerodynamics without exceeding the computational budget of real-time nonlinear optimization. Here, we propose a compact, continuously differentiable model that captures the dominant couplings of bird-scale ornithopters, enabling real-time predictive control. We validated the method with the XFly ornithopter flying along circular and three-dimensional racing trajectories and achieved a mean deviation from the reference trajectory between 6.5 and 9 cm at speeds up to 3 m/s, which represents an almost 10-fold improvement over prior ornithopter control methods.

</details>


### [164] [Monitoring autonomous persistent surveillance missions using invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06062)
*Vladislav Nenchev,Prodromos Sotiriadis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究当自治系统为黑箱时，用于持续监视任务的运行时监控方法，提出了一种基于离线计算不变量的组合式监控器，并在真实机器人迷宫中验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以处理黑箱自治系统下的持续监视任务中大规模空间的运行时监控问题，因为全系统不变量难以计算。

Method: 将环境划分为有限区域，每个区域携带不确定性状态；将闭环系统建模为依赖状态的线性参数变化混合系统；离线计算不变量；提出通过分散计算每个不确定性区域的低维不变量集并在线检查其合取的组合式监控方法。

Result: 在真实机器人持续监视迷宫的案例研究中验证了该方法的实用性；在常见独立性假设下，组合式监控器相对于全系统不变量是完备且可靠的。

Conclusion: 组合式监控方法能有效解决大规模监视空间中不变量难以计算的问题，且具有实际可部署性。

Abstract: This paper studies runtime monitoring for persistent surveillance by autonomous robots when the autonomy stack is a black box. The environment is partitioned into finitely many parts, each carrying an uncertainty state that decreases when observed and increases otherwise. We model the closed loop as a state-dependent hybrid system with linear parameter varying dynamics and design a monitor based on an invariant computed offline. As this invariant is typically hard to obtain for large to-be-surveyed spaces, we propose a compositional monitor obtained by decentralized computation of low-dimensional invariant sets for each uncertainty region, and checking their conjunction online. Under common independence assumptions, the compositional monitor is sound and complete with respect to the full-system invariant. The approach is applied in a case study with a real robot persistently monitoring a labyrinth, emphasizing its applicability in practice.

</details>


### [165] [When to Trust Imagination: Adaptive Action Execution for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06222)
*Rui Wang,Yue Zhang,Jiehong Lin,Kuncheng Luo,Jianan Wang,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FFDC，一种轻量级验证器，通过联合推理预测动作、预测视觉动态、真实观测和语言指令，自适应调整World Action Model的执行块大小，在保持长期执行效率的同时恢复对接触密集或困难阶段的响应能力，在RoboTwin基准上减少69.10%的WAM前向传播和34.02%的执行时间，同时成功率提升2.54%，真实世界实验成功率提升35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前World Action Models在执行时采用固定数量的预测动作，导致机器人无法感知预测未来与实际物理展开之间的一致性，在接触密集或困难阶段缺乏响应能力。

Method: 提出Future Forward Dynamics Causal Attention (FFDC)，一种轻量级验证器，联合推理预测动作、预测视觉动态、真实观测和语言指令，评估剩余动作序列的可信度；并引入Mixture-of-Horizon Training提升长视野轨迹覆盖。

Result: 在RoboTwin基准上，WAM前向传播减少69.10%，执行时间减少34.02%，成功率提升2.54%；真实世界实验成功率提升35%。

Conclusion: FFDC实现了预测-观测一致性驱动的自适应动作块大小，在保证长期执行效率的同时增强了困难阶段的响应能力，取得了鲁棒性与效率的强平衡。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by jointly predicting future visual observations and future actions. However, current WAMs typically execute a fixed number of predicted actions after each model inference, leaving the robot blind to whether the imagined future remains consistent with the actual physical rollout. In this work, we formulate adaptive WAM execution as a future-reality verification problem: the robot should execute longer when the WAM-predicted future remains reliable, and replan earlier when reality deviates from imagination. To this end, we propose Future Forward Dynamics Causal Attention (FFDC), a lightweight verifier that jointly reasons over predicted future actions, predicted visual dynamics, real observations, and language instructions to estimate whether the remaining action rollout can still be trusted. FFDC enables adaptive action chunk sizes as an emergent consequence of prediction-observation consistency, preserving the efficiency of long-horizon execution while restoring responsiveness in contact-rich or difficult phases. We further introduce Mixture-of-Horizon Training to improve long-horizon trajectory coverage for adaptive execution. Experiments on the RoboTwin benchmark and in the real world demonstrate that our method achieves a strong robustness-efficiency trade-off: on RoboTwin, it reduces WAM forward passes by 69.10% and execution time by 34.02%, while improving success rate by 2.54% over the short-chunk baseline; in real-world experiments, it improves success rate by 35%.

</details>


### [166] [RobotEQ: Transitioning from Passive Intelligence to Active Intelligence in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06234)
*Kuofei Fang,Xinyi Che,Haomin Ouyang,Shufan Zhang,Xuehao Wang,Qi Liu,Liyi Liu,Chenqi Zhang,Wenxi Cai,Wenyu Dai,Jinyang Wu,Fan Zhang,Haoyu Chen,Bin He,Zheng Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RobotEQ，首个面向具身AI主动智能的基准测试，通过1,900张第一人称图像、5,353个动作判断问题和1,286个空间定位问题，评估现有模型在具身场景中理解和遵守社会规范的能力。实验表明当前模型在主动智能上表现不足，尤其是空间定位，而RAG技术引入外部社会规范知识可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI研究聚焦于基于显式指令的任务完成，但机器人融入人类社会需要理解哪些行为是允许或禁止的（即使没有明确指令）。本文区分了用户引导的被动智能与无引导的主动智能，旨在评估模型能否在具身场景中理解并遵守社会规范。

Method: 构建RobotEQ-Data数据集（1,900张第一人称图像，10个具身类别、56个子类别，5,353个动作判断问题及1,286个空间定位问题），并设立RobotEQ-Bench评估框架，系统测试现有模型在主动智能任务上的表现。

Result: 当前模型在实现可靠的主动智能方面表现不足，尤其在空间定位任务上差距明显；引入RAG技术利用外部社会规范知识库能够普遍提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 该工作可推动机器人从用户引导的被动操控向主动社会合规转型，为机器人社会融入提供基础评估工具。

Abstract: Embodied AI is a prominent research topic in both academia and industry. Current research centers on completing tasks based on explicit user instructions. However, for robots to integrate into human society, they must understand which actions are permissible and which are prohibited, even without explicit commands. We refer to the user-guided AI as passive intelligence and the unguided AI as active intelligence. This paper introduces RobotEQ, the first benchmark for active intelligence, aiming to assess whether existing models can comprehend and adhere to social norms in embodied scenarios. First, we construct RobotEQ-Data, a dataset consisting of 1,900 egocentric images, spanning 10 representative embodied categories and 56 subcategories. Through extensive manual annotation, we provide 5,353 action judgment questions and 1,286 spatial grounding questions, specifying appropriate robot actions across diverse scenarios. Furthermore, we establish RobotEQ-Bench to evaluate the performance of state-of-the-art models on this task. Experimental results show that current models still fall short in achieving reliable active intelligence, particularly in spatial grounding. Meanwhile, we observe that leveraging RAG techniques to incorporate external social norm knowledge bases can generally enhance performance. This work can facilitate the transition of robotics from user-guided passive manipulation to active social compliance.

</details>


### [167] [CKT-WAM: Parameter-Efficient Context Knowledge Transfer Between World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06247)
*Yuhua Jiang,Yijun Guo,Hongbing Yang,Guojun Lei,Nuo Chen,Yinuo Zhang,Shaoqiang Yan,Bo Lin,Feifei Gao,Biqing Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CKT-WAM框架，通过紧凑的文本嵌入空间上下文，将教师世界动作模型的知识高效迁移到学生模型中，仅需1.17%可训练参数即可达到86.1%的总成功率，在仿真和真实世界长时程操控任务中均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构世界动作模型之间存在潜在接口不匹配、高适应成本和传统蒸馏目标过于刚性等问题，导致知识迁移困难。现有方法依赖输出模仿或密集隐状态匹配，效率低下且扩展性差。

Method: CKT-WAM提取教师模型的中间隐状态，通过压缩器的可学习查询交叉注意力（LQCA）减少token数量，再经过常开通用适配器、轻量级路由器和稀疏激活的专用适配器进行变换，最终将生成的上下文嵌入附加到学生模型的条件文本嵌入中，以最小架构改动注入迁移知识。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus上达到86.1%总成功率，仅使用1.17%可训练参数；在真实世界四类多步长时程任务中达到83.3%平均成功率，均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: CKT-WAM通过参数高效的上下文知识迁移方式，在零样本泛化和整体性能上均有显著提升，且具备良好的真实世界迁移能力，验证了文本嵌入空间作为知识迁移媒介的有效性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) provide a powerful generative framework for embodied control, yet transferring knowledge across heterogeneous WAMs remains challenging due to mismatched latent interfaces, high adaptation cost, and the rigidity of conventional distillation objectives. We propose \textbf{CKT-WAM}, a parameter-efficient \textbf{C}ontext \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{T}ransfer framework that transfers teacher WAM's knowledge into a student WAM through a compact context in the text embedding space, rather than output imitation or dense hidden-state matching. Specifically, CKT-WAM extracts intermediate teacher hidden states, reduces the number of tokens via compressors' learnable-query cross attention (LQCA), and transforms them through an always-on generalized adapter, a lightweight router, and sparsely activated specialized adapters. The resulting context is then appended to the student's conditioning textual embeddings, thereby injecting the transferred knowledge into the student with minimal architectural modification. Experiments show that CKT-WAM consistently improves zero-shot generalization and achieves the best overall performance on LIBERO-Plus, reaching 86.1\% total success rate with only 1.17\% trainable parameters, while approaching full fine-tuning performance. Beyond simulation, CKT-WAM also demonstrates strong real-world long-horizon manipulation ability, achieving the best average success rate of 83.3\% across four multi-step and long-horizon tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/YuhuaJiang2002/CKT-WAM.

</details>


### [168] [Toward Visually Realistic Simulation: A Benchmark for Evaluating Robot Manipulation in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06311)
*Yixin Zhu,Zixiong Wang,Jian Yang,Jin Xie,Jingyi Yu,Jiayuan Gu,Beibei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VISER是一个高视觉真实感的机器人操作仿真评估基准，包含1000+基于物理渲染的3D资产和多样化任务，通过缩小仿真与现实的视觉域差距，实现了高达0.92的皮尔逊相关系数，能更可靠地预测真实世界中的策略表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作仿真基准虽然覆盖了多样化的任务类别，但缺乏视觉真实感，导致仿真与现实之间存在较大的视觉域差距，从而削弱了仿真评估对真实世界性能预测的可靠性。

Method: 首先通过系统分析分离光照和材质对几何推理与空间定位的影响。然后构建VISER基准：包含1000+具有PBR材质的高保真3D资产数据集；提出利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）进行材质感知部件分割和材质检索的自动化管道，实现可扩展的物理合理资产生成；基于该数据集构建抓取、放置和长时序等多样化评估任务。

Result: VISER基准在不同策略上的仿真与真实世界性能之间实现了强相关性，平均皮尔逊相关系数达到0.92。

Conclusion: 光照和材质等视觉因素在机器人操作的几何推理和空间定位中起关键作用但常被忽视；通过提升仿真视觉真实感能显著增强仿真评估对现实世界性能的预测可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable simulation evaluation of robot manipulation policies serves as a high-fidelity proxy for real-world performance. Although existing benchmarks cover a wide range of task categories, they lack visual realism, creating a large domain gap between simulation and reality. This undermines the reliability of simulation-based evaluation in predicting real-world performance. To mitigate the sim-to-real visual gap, we conduct a systematic analysis to isolate the effects of lighting and material. Our results show that these factors play a critical role in geometric reasoning and spatial grounding, yet are largely overlooked in existing benchmarks. Motivated by the analysis, we propose VISER, a visually realistic benchmark for evaluating robot manipulation in simulation. VISER features a high-fidelity dataset of over 1,000 3D assets with physically-based rendering (PBR) materials, along with 3D scenes created from these assets through curated layouts or generation. To this end, we propose an automated pipeline leveraging Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for material-aware part segmentation and material retrieval, enabling scalable generation of physically plausible assets. Building on the high-fidelity 3D asset dataset, we construct diverse evaluation tasks, such as grasping, placing, and long-horizon tasks, enabling scalable and reproducible assessment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. Our benchmark shows a strong correlation between simulation and real-world performance, achieving an average Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.92 across different policies.

</details>


### [169] [AssistDLO: Assistive Teleoperation for Deformable Linear Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06323)
*Berk Guler,Simon Manschitz,Kay Pompetzki,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AssistDLO是一种面向可变形线性物体（DLO）遥操作的辅助框架，结合多视图状态估计、视觉辅助和基于控制障碍函数的几何感知共享自主控制器（SA-CBF）。在双手解绳结用户实验（N=22）中，SA-CBF能显著提升新手成功率（71%→88%），但辅助效果高度依赖操作者专业水平和DLO材料属性，表明需要自适应、用户感知和材料感知的共享自主策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLO操作面临无限维构型空间和复杂非线性动力学挑战，遥操作中深度不确定性阻碍状态感知与反应，传统共享自主方法依赖简单几何吸引子且无法保持DLO几何形态。

Method: 提出AssistDLO框架，包含实时多视图状态估计、视觉辅助（VA）和基于控制障碍函数的几何感知共享自主控制器（SA-CBF），后者作为几何感知漏斗实现精确抓取同时保留操作者高层自主权。

Result: SA-CBF将新手成功率从71%提升至88%，对较硬绳索效果显著；专家用户偏好视觉辅助；高柔顺长绳索更受益于视觉支持而非局部动作辅助。

Conclusion: 有效DLO遥操作不能依赖固定策略，需要自适应、用户感知和材料感知的共享自主方法。

Abstract: Manipulating Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) is challenging in robotics due to their infinite-dimensional configuration space and complex nonlinear dynamics. In teleoperation, depth uncertainty hinders state perception and reaction. AssistDLO addresses this challenge as an assistive teleoperation framework for DLO manipulation that combines real-time multi-view state estimation, visual assistance (VA), and a geometry-aware shared-autonomy controller based on Control Barrier Functions (SA-CBF). While traditional shared autonomy methods often rely on simple geometric attractors and may fail to preserve DLO geometry, SA-CBF acts as a geometry-aware funnel, facilitating precise grasping while preserving the operator's high-level authority. The framework is evaluated in a bimanual knot-untangling user study (N = 22) using ropes with varying length and rigidity. Results show that the effectiveness of the assistance depends strongly on operator expertise and DLO properties. SA-CBF provides the strongest gains for naive users, acting as a skill equalizer that increases task success from 71% to 88%, and is effective for stiffer ropes. Conversely, expert users prefer VA, and highly compliant, long ropes benefit more from visual support than localized action assistance. Ultimately, these findings demonstrate that effective DLO teleoperation cannot rely on a fixed strategy, highlighting the critical need for adaptive, user-aware, and material-aware shared autonomy.

</details>


### [170] [TouchDrive: Electronics-Free Tactile Sensing Interface for Assistive Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06432)
*Jing Xu,Xuezhi Niu,Didem Gurdur Broo,Klas Hjort*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TouchDrive是一种低成本、无电子元件的触觉传感接口，通过气动阀门将接触力直接转换为气动反馈，帮助用户调节抓取力，实现对易碎和柔软物体的精细操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助抓取系统依赖电子传感和多阶段处理流程，系统复杂度高、可及性差，限制了其在辅助抓取中的实用性和可部署性。

Method: 利用气动常闭阀、压缩空气罐、传感元件和触觉反馈致动器构建纯无源机械回路，将接触力直接转化为气动反馈，实现感知、信号生成与反馈一体化。

Result: 在多种机器人平台上验证，能稳定操作易碎和柔软物体（水果等日常物品达20种），实现可靠抓取性能。

Conclusion: TouchDrive通过无电子元件的纯气动反馈方案，降低系统复杂度与成本，提升了辅助抓取的可及性和实用性，适用于精细操作任务。

Abstract: Assistive robotic grasping plays an important role in enabling safe and adaptive manipulation of diverse objects. However, existing systems often rely on electronic sensing and multi-stage processing pipelines, increasing system complexity and reducing accessibility. To address these limitations, we present TouchDrive, a cost-effective, electronics-free tactile sensing interface for assistive grasping. TouchDrive directly converts contact forces into pneumatic feedback through valve-mediated switching, integrating sensing, signal generation, and feedback within a single passive mechanical loop. The system can be employed using a pneumatic normally closed valve, a compressed air tank, sensing element, and haptic feedback actuator without electronics. By delivering tactile cues, TouchDrive empowers users to modulate grasp forces, enabling precise and robust delicate manipulation of compliant and fragile objects. The interface has been validated across diverse robotic platforms, consistently demonstrating reliable performance and practical applicability in assistive grasping tasks, such as handling fruits and everyday items (up to 20 objects).

</details>


### [171] [GA3T: A Ground-Aerial Terrain Traversability Dataset for Heterogeneous Robot Teams in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06478)
*Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Quan Tran,Christian Ricks,Dhanush Parthasarathy,Amir Kaidarov,Neil Deshpande,Sukaina Najm,David Han,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GA3T是一个真实世界中的异构空地机器人协同感知数据集，包含超过13,000帧同步数据（约29分钟），涉及地面机器人（3D LiDAR、立体相机、IMU、GPS）和空中无人机（RGB、热红外、GPS）在森林小径、岩石路、泥地、雪地和草地等非结构化地形中的多模态观测，并包含SAM 3零样本分割和8,000多张人工标注图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏真实世界多机器人协同感知数据集，尤其是非结构化地形中空地平台具备重叠多模态观测的数据集，制约了异构空地机器人团队在复杂户外环境中的感知能力提升。

Method: 使用Clearpath Husky UGV和Autel EVO II UAV在4种非结构化环境（森林小径、岩石路、泥地、雪地、草地）中采集数据，地面平台提供3D LiDAR、立体相机、IMU和GPS，空中平台提供RGB、热红外和GPS，共记录约29分钟、13,000+同步帧，并采用SAM 3零样本分割和人工标注。

Result: 构建了包含多模态、跨视角观测的真实空地协同感知数据集，早春采集期间稀疏树冠使得空中机器人能部分透过树木观测地面机器人和地形，支持遮挡感知的协同感知。

Conclusion: GA3T数据集专为支持跨视角感知、空地视角融合、可通行性估计和真实越野环境中的协同场景理解等研究而设计，弥补了现有多机器人数据集聚焦SLAM或模拟协同驾驶的不足。

Abstract: Heterogeneous air-ground robot teams combine complementary sensing modalities, mobility characteristics, and spatial viewpoints that can significantly enhance perception in complex outdoor environments. However, progress in multi-robot collaborative perception has been constrained by the lack of real-world datasets featuring overlapping multi-modal observations from platforms operating in unstructured terrain. We present GA3T (Ground-Aerial Team for Terrain Traversal), a real-world multi-robot collaborative perception dataset collected using a Clearpath Husky UGV and an Autel EVO~II UAV across diverse unstructured environments, including forest trails, rocky paths, muddy terrain, snow piles, and grass-covered fields. The ground platform provides 3D LiDAR, stereo camera, IMU, and GPS data, while the aerial platform contributes RGB imagery, thermal/infrared observations, and GPS from a complementary overhead viewpoint, allowing for rich cross-modal and cross-view perception. The dataset is collected in 4 unique environments, with over 13,000 synchronized frames across approximately 29 minutes of operation, and includes both SAM~3-based zero-shot segmentation and over 8,000 manually labeled images. A unique aspect of the dataset is its early-spring collection period, during which sparse tree canopies allow the aerial robot to partially observe the ground robot and terrain through the trees, allowing for occlusion-aware collaborative perception. Unlike prior multi-robot datasets that focus on SLAM or simulated cooperative driving, GA3T is specifically designed to support research on cross-view perception, air-ground viewpoint fusion, traversability estimation, and collaborative scene understanding in real off-road environments.

</details>


### [172] [OA-WAM: Object-Addressable World Action Model for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06481)
*Yushan Liu,Peibo Sun,Shoujie Li,Yifan Xie,Lingfeng Zhang,Xintao Chao,Shiyuan Dong,Fang Chen,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OA-WAM，一种可寻址对象的世界动作模型，通过将场景分解为可持久化寻址的槽状态（机器人槽+对象槽），在保持世界预测能力的同时实现对特定对象的鲁棒操作，在LIBERO和SimplerEnv上达到SOTA性能，且槽干预测试显示强可寻址性（cosine=0.87 vs ≤0.09）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）将预测世界表示为整体图像、视频令牌或全局潜变量，当指令指向特定对象时，动作解码器难以处理此类表示，尤其在场景偏移下对象身份与环境纠缠不清，导致操作鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将每帧分解为N+1个槽状态（1个机器人槽+N个对象槽），每个槽包含持久化寻址向量与时变内容向量；与文本、图像、本体感知、历史动作令牌以块因果序列融合；世界头预测下一帧槽状态，流匹配动作头解码16步连续动作块；通过跨槽注意力仅基于寻址键路由、每层重置寻址切片实现可寻址性，无需额外令牌。

Result: LIBERO达97.8%，SimplerEnv达79.3%；在LIBERO-Plus几何轴上达到SOTA；因果槽干预测试的swap-binding cosine为0.87，而整体基线最高仅0.09。

Conclusion: 可寻址对象状态为场景扰动下的鲁棒世界-动作建模提供了有效接口。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enhance Vision-Language-Action policies by jointly predicting scene evolution and robot actions, but existing methods usually represent the predicted world as holistic images, video tokens, or global latents. These representations are difficult for an action decoder to address when an instruction refers to a particular object, especially under scene shifts where object identity is entangled with context. We propose OA-WAM, an Object-Addressable World Action Model for robust robot manipulation. OA-WAM decomposes each frame into N+1 slot states, with one robot slot and N object slots. Each slot contains a persistent address vector and a time-varying content vector, and is fused with text, image, proprioception, and past-action tokens in a block-causal sequence. A world head predicts next-frame slot states, while a flow-matching action head decodes a 16-step continuous action chunk in the same forward pass. Addressability is enforced by routing cross-slot attention through address-only keys and resetting the address slice at every transformer layer, separating which object to act on from what that object currently is without adding extra tokens. OA-WAM matches strong VLA and WAM baselines on LIBERO (97.8%) and SimplerEnv (79.3%), reaches state-of-the-art performance on the most relevant LIBERO-Plus geometric axes, and remains competitive on the seven-axis aggregate. A causal slot-intervention test yields a swap-binding cosine of 0.87, versus at most 0.09 for holistic baselines. These results suggest that addressable object states provide an effective interface for robust world-action modeling under scene perturbations.

</details>


### [173] [Lie Group Formulation of Recursive Dynamics Algorithms of Higher Order for Floating-Base Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06498)
*Ahmed Ali,Chiara Gabellieri,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了浮基树形结构（基座位姿在SE(3)上演化，附属机构为T^{n1}×R^{n2}构型空间的开链）的Lie群牛顿-欧拉、刚体惯性张量及混合动力学算法的高阶时间导数计算方法，并证明了闭式运动方程中可容许科里奥利矩阵满足无源性，且刚体惯性张量在各阶导数下保持不变。在12自由度空中机械臂上的数值实验验证了高达五阶的动力学推导，基准测试表明计算成本随导数阶次呈二次增长，而自动微分基线呈指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浮基机器人动力学算法通常仅计算一阶导数（如速度和加速度），缺乏系统的高阶时间导数计算方法，而高阶导数对于模型预测控制、轨迹优化和灵敏度分析等应用至关重要。

Method: 利用空间旋量表示，在SE(3)和T^{n1}×R^{n2}构型空间上，推导Lie群牛顿-欧拉、刚体惯性张量和混合动力学递归算法的高阶时间导数，并将递归整合为闭式运动方程，识别满足无源性的科里奥利矩阵，证明刚体惯性张量在各阶导数下不变。

Result: 在12自由度空中机械臂上成功验证了高达五阶的动力学推导，基准测试显示所提方法的计算成本随导数阶次呈二次方增长，而自动微分基线呈指数增长。

Conclusion: 所提出的高阶时间导数动力学算法在计算效率上显著优于自动微分方法，可有效支撑需要高阶动力学信息的机器人控制与规划任务。

Abstract: In this paper, we describe procedures for computing higher-order time derivatives of the Lie-group Newton-Euler, Articulated-Body Inertia, and hybrid dynamics algorithms for floating-base trees, where the base configuration evolves on SE(3) and the attached mechanism is an open kinematic tree with configuration on the (n1+n2)-dimensional manifold T^{n1} \times R^{n2}, using spatial representation of twists. After presenting the algorithms, we collect the resulting recursions into closed-form equations of motion, identifying an admissible Coriolis matrix satisfying the passivity property, and showing that the articulated inertia tensor remains unchanged across all time derivatives. We then apply the developed methods to a 12-DoF aerial manipulator to derive analytical expressions for its geometric forward and inverse dynamics along with their first time derivatives whereas the numerical simulations successfully evaluate these dynamics up to fifth order. Finally, to demonstrate their practical utility, we benchmark the proposed extensions and show that, in the considered tests, their computational cost scales quadratically with the derivative order, whereas the automatic-differentiation baseline exhibits exponential scaling.

</details>


### [174] [ReActor: Reinforcement Learning for Physics-Aware Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06593)
*David Müller,Agon Serifi,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种双层优化框架，将人体运动重定向到机器人形态，同时训练跟踪策略，生成物理一致的运动以促进模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将人体运动重定向到机器人时存在脚滑、自碰撞、动力学不可行等问题，阻碍了下游的模仿学习。

Method: 采用双层优化框架，联合优化运动重定向和强化学习跟踪策略训练；通过推导上层损失的近似梯度使优化可解；只需稀疏的语义刚体对应关系，自动调参。

Result: 在仿真和真实硬件上验证了方法有效性，成功将人体运动重定向到形态差异显著的机器人（包括四足机器人）上。

Conclusion: 将重定向与物理仿真直接集成可以生成物理上合理的运动，促进鲁棒的模仿学习，且无需手动调参即可保持不同形态间的特征运动。

Abstract: Retargeting human kinematic reference motion onto a robot's morphology remains a formidable challenge. Existing methods often produce physical inconsistencies, such as foot sliding, self-collisions, or dynamically infeasible motions, which hinder downstream imitation learning. We propose a bilevel optimization framework that jointly adapts reference motions to a robot's morphology while training a tracking policy using reinforcement learning. To make the optimization tractable, we derive an approximate gradient for the upper-level loss. Our framework requires only a sparse set of semantic rigid-body correspondences and eliminates the need for manual tuning by identifying optimal values for a parameterization expressive enough to preserve characteristic motion across different embodiments. Moreover, by integrating retargeting directly with physics simulation, we produce physically plausible motions that facilitate robust imitation learning. We validate our method in simulation and on hardware, demonstrating challenging motions for morphologies that differ significantly from a human, including retargeting onto a quadruped.

</details>


### [175] [Cross-Modal Navigation with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06595)
*Shuo Liu,Xinzichen Li,Christopher Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CRONA框架，通过多智能体强化学习实现跨模态协作导航，实验表明该方法在性能、效率和多模态利用上优于单智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量多模态对齐数据难以获取，单模型处理多模态输入会导致表示复杂化、策略空间膨胀，需要可扩展的跨模态协作范式。

Method: 基于MARL的CRONA框架，利用控制相关的辅助信念和带全局状态的集中式多模态评论家来提升智能体间协作。

Result: 多智能体方法在视觉-声学导航任务上显著优于单智能体基线；同质协作适用于短程导航，异质协作普遍高效，复杂大环境需更丰富的多模态感知和更大模型容量。

Conclusion: 轻量级模态专业化智能体间的跨模态协作是一种可扩展、灵活且有效的导航范式，CRONA通过MARL有效提升了协作效率。

Abstract: Robust embodied navigation relies on complementary sensory cues. However, high-quality and well-aligned multi-modal data is often difficult to obtain in practice. Training a monolithic model is also challenging as rich multi-modal inputs induce complex representations and substantially enlarge the policy space. Cross-modal collaboration among lightweight modality-specialized agents offers a scalable paradigm. It enables flexible deployment and parallel execution, while preserving the strength of each modality. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CRONA}, a Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for \textbf{Cro}ss-Modal \textbf{Na}vigation. CRONA improves collaboration by leveraging control-relevant auxiliary beliefs and a centralized multi-modal critic with global state. Experiments on visual-acoustic navigation tasks show that multi-agent methods significantly improve performance and efficiency over single-agent baselines. We find that homogeneous collaboration with limited modalities is sufficient for short-range navigation under salient cues; heterogeneous collaboration among agents with complementary modalities is generally efficient and effective; and navigation in large, complex environments requires both richer multi-modal perception and increased model capacity.

</details>


### [176] [Multi-Robot Coordination in V2X Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06662)
*John Pravin Arockiasamy,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向社交机器人的V2X通信框架，通过机器人感知服务(RAS)和机器人机动协调服务(RMCS)实现去中心化多机器人协作，在真实场景中演示了人形机器人和四足机器人的协同过马路，仿真实验证明了该方法在混合V2X环境下的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的V2X通信标准主要针对传统车辆设计，缺乏对社交机器人（如人形机器人、四足机器人）在复杂城市交通环境中去中心化协作的支持，亟需一个与ETSI标准对齐的机器人专用通信框架。

Method: 基于ETSI Cooperative Awareness和Maneuver Coordination服务，提出两个机器人设施层服务：RAS（通过RAM消息实现角色感知、任务导向的机器人感知，并集成外部检测的弱势道路使用者）和RMCS（通过RMCM消息实现事件驱动的低延迟机动协调）。采用形式化有限状态协调模型，结合真实场景验证与仿真评估。

Result: 真实场景中成功实现人形机器人与四足机器人协同辅助行人过马路的确定性多机器人协调；仿真表明RAS聚类可有效整合非V2X弱势道路使用者、减少V2X使能节点的冗余传输，从而降低信道负载。

Conclusion: 提出的RAS和RMCS服务为将协作机器人集成到未来互联、协作、自动化出行生态系统提供了可扩展且与标准兼容的基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication framework that enables decentralized cooperation among social robots operating in complex urban traffic environments. Building on ETSI Cooperative Awareness and Maneuver Coordination services, the framework introduces two robot-centric facility-layer services: the Robot Awareness Service (RAS) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Service (RMCS), realized through the Robot Awareness Message (RAM) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Message (RMCM), respectively. RAS enables role-aware, task-oriented robot awareness while integrating externally detected Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs), including non-V2X pedestrians, into cooperative awareness. RMCS supports event-driven, low-latency coordination of robot maneuvers under explicitly established roles, without centralized infrastructure or prior pairing. A real-world proof of concept demonstrates deterministic multi-robot coordination between a humanoid robot and a quadrupedal robot assisting a pedestrian during a road-crossing scenario, governed by a formally specified finite-state coordination model. Complementary simulations evaluate robot-mediated VRU clustering in mixed V2X environments, showing that RAS-based clustering integrates non-V2X VRUs in safety-critical areas while reducing redundant transmissions from V2X-enabled VRUs, thereby lowering channel load. Together, the proposed services provide a scalable and standards-aligned foundation for integrating cooperative robots into future Connected, Cooperative, and Automated Mobility ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Annotator Policy Models (APMs)，一种从标注行为中学习标注者内部安全策略的可解释模型，无需额外标注成本即可揭示标注分歧的根源（操作失误、政策模糊或价值多元），支持更有针对性和包容性的安全政策设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全策略依赖数据标注，但标注分歧普遍存在且来源多样（操作失误、政策模糊、价值多元），而直接询问标注者推理过程成本高且不可靠，亟需一种低成本、可靠的方法来区分分歧来源。

Method: 提出Annotator Policy Models (APMs)，从标注行为数据中学习标注者内部安全策略的可解释模型，无需额外标注工作；通过预测反事实编辑响应和恢复已知政策差异进行验证。

Result: APMs准确建模标注者安全策略（>80%准确率），忠实预测反事实编辑响应，并在受控设置下恢复已知政策差异。在LLM和人类标注上成功揭示政策模糊和价值多元现象。

Conclusion: APMs能够在不增加标注负担的情况下，使标注者推理过程可见可比较，支持更有针对性、透明和包容的安全政策设计。

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [178] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZAYA1-8B是一个基于MoE++架构的推理聚焦混合专家模型，拥有7亿活跃参数和80亿总参数。在完整AMD平台上完成预训练、中期训练和监督微调。以不到10亿活跃参数在数学和编程基准上匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528，并通过四阶段强化学习级联和Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，在AIME'25达到91.9%、HMMT'25达到89.6%，缩小了与更大模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在资源受限（小于10亿活跃参数）条件下，通过架构创新（MoE++）、训练策略优化（推理数据预训练、四阶段RL级联）和测试时计算方法（Markovian RSA），使小模型在数学推理和代码生成等挑战性任务上达到与大模型竞争的水平。

Method: 1) 基于MoE++架构构建ZAYA1-8B（7亿活跃/80亿总参数）；2) 在全栈AMD计算平台上完成预训练、中期训练和SFT；3) 从预训练阶段就引入推理数据，使用保持答案的修剪方案；4) 采用四阶段强化学习级联：推理热身→400任务RLVE-Gym课程→数学和代码RL（结合测试时计算轨迹和合成代码环境）→行为RL；5) 提出Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，递归聚合并行推理轨迹，仅保留有限长度的推理尾部。

Result: ZAYA1-8B在多个数学和编程基准上匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528；结合Markovian RSA后在AIME'25达到91.9%、HMMT'25达到89.6%，仅需携带4K token的推理尾部，显著缩小了与Gemini-2.5 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5-High等大模型的差距。

Conclusion: 通过架构、训练策略和测试时计算的协同优化，小参数模型能够在推理密集型任务上取得与大模型竞争的性能，验证了推理能力可以从预训练阶段开始系统性地培养。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-8B, a reasoning-focused mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 700M active and 8B total parameters, built on Zyphra's MoE++ architecture. ZAYA1-8B's core pretraining, midtraining, and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) were performed on a full-stack AMD compute, networking, and software platform. With under 1B active parameters, ZAYA1-8B matches or exceeds DeepSeek-R1-0528 on several challenging mathematics and coding benchmarks, and remains competitive with substantially larger open-weight reasoning models. ZAYA1-8B was trained from scratch for reasoning, with reasoning data included from pretraining onward using an answer-preserving trimming scheme. Post-training uses a four-stage RL cascade: reasoning warmup on math and puzzles; a 400-task RLVE-Gym curriculum; math and code RL with test-time compute traces and synthetic code environments built from competitive-programming references; and behavioral RL for chat and instruction following. We also introduce Markovian RSA, a test-time compute method that recursively aggregates parallel reasoning traces while carrying forward only bounded-length reasoning tails between rounds. In TTC evaluation, Markovian RSA raises ZAYA1-8B to 91.9\% on AIME'25 and 89.6\% on HMMT'25 while carrying forward only a 4K-token tail, narrowing the gap to much larger reasoning models including Gemini-2.5 Pro, DeepSeek-V3.2, and GPT-5-High.

</details>


### [179] [Intelligent CCTV for Urban Design: AI-Based Analysis of Soft Infrastructure at Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05402)
*Vinit Katariya,Seungjin Kim,Curtis Craig,Nichole Morris,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于AI的分析框架，利用现有CCTV基础设施评估软性干预措施（如临时行人避难岛和路缘扩展）对车辆速度和安全的影响。在明尼阿波利斯的实验表明，无信号交叉口的平均速度和85百分位速度分别降低18.75%和16.56%，通过交通量减少12.2%；信号化交叉口平均速度和85百分位速度分别降低20.0%和17.19%。证明了软性基础设施的交通 calming 效果以及AI方法在低成本、快速、循证交通政策评估中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通数据采集成本高、效率低，且缺乏对软性基础设施干预措施（如临时行人避难岛和路缘扩展）进行快速、低成本效果评估的方法。

Method: 利用现有CCTV基础设施，结合深度学习和基于透视的速度估计方法，构建AI分析框架。在干预前和干预后第1周、第2周重复监测驾驶员行为，对比无信号交叉口和信号化交叉口的速度与交通量变化。

Result: 无信号交叉口：平均速度降低18.75%，85百分位速度降低16.56%，通过交通量减少12.2%。信号化交叉口：平均速度降低20.0%，85百分位速度降低17.19%（除一个地点外）。

Conclusion: 软性基础设施具有显著的交通 calming 效果；AI驱动的分析方法能够实现快速、低成本、基于证据的交通政策评估，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and computer vision are transforming transportation data collection. This study introduces an AI-enabled analytics framework leveraging existing CCTV infrastructure to evaluate the impact of soft interventions, such as temporary pedestrian refuges and curb extensions, on vehicle speed and safety. Using deep learning and perspective-based speed estimation, we evaluated driver behavior before and after interventions, with repeated post-installation monitoring in Week 1 and Week 2, in Minneapolis. Findings reveal that at unsignalized intersections, mean and 85th-percentile speeds fell by up to 18.75% and 16.56%, respectively, while pass-through traffic decreased by as much as 12.2%. Signalized intersections showed comparable reductions except one location, with mean and 85th-percentile speeds dropping by up to 20.0% and 17.19%. These results demonstrate the traffic-calming effectiveness of soft infrastructure and underscore the utility of AI-powered methods for rapid, low-cost, and evidence-based transport policy evaluation.

</details>


### [180] [When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
*Jiechen Li,Catherine A. Barry,Rishika Randev,Janet Chen,Ella Jorgensen,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了LLM中的谄媚（sycophancy）是社会对齐与 epistemic 完整性之间的边界失效，并提出一个包含三个条件（用户线索、对齐行为、 epistemic 受损）的框架和分类体系来界定谄媚现象，最终讨论了边界感知评估与缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将谄媚操作化为简单的外部行为（如同意不正确观点、立场反转等），仅捕捉了显性形式，而忽略了涉及 epistemic 完整性和社会对齐的更微妙的边界失效，因此需要更精确的理论框架来界定谄媚的本质。

Method: 提出了一个三条件框架：1) 用户表达线索（信念、偏好或自我概念）；2) 模型通过对齐行为向该线索偏移；3) 这种偏移损害了 epistemic 准确性、独立推理或适当纠正。同时引入了一个包含对齐目标、机制和严重性三个维度的谄媚分类法。

Result: 构建了更精确的谄媚概念框架，区分了"同意"与"谄媚"（后者是对齐行为取代了独立 epistemic 判断），并提出了边界感知评估、结构化评分标准和缓解策略等实践建议。

Conclusion: 论文主张将谄媚重新理解为社会对齐与 epistemic 完整性之间的边界失效，而非简单的同意行为，并呼吁采用边界感知的评估方法来改进对齐评估。

Abstract: This position paper argues that sycophancy in LLMs is a boundary failure between social alignment and epistemic integrity. Existing work often operationalizes sycophancy through external behavior such as agreement with incorrect user beliefs, position reversals, or deviation from an objective standard of correctness. These formulations capture only overt forms of the phenomenon and leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified. We argue that sycophancy should not be understood as agreement alone, but as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment. To clarify this boundary, we propose a three-condition framework for sycophancy. First, the user expresses a cue in the form of a belief, preference, or self-concept. Second, the model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior. Third, this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. We also introduce a taxonomy for classifying sycophancy, consisting of alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity. The paper concludes by discussing implications for alignment evaluation and argues for boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies, while situating these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

</details>


### [181] [LaTA: A Drop-in, FERPA-Compliant Local-LLM Autograder for Upper-Division STEM Coursework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05410)
*Jesse A. Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaTA是一个完全在本地硬件上运行的开源LaTeX自动评分系统，采用四阶段流水线和本地托管的链式思维LLM（120B参数）进行评分。在俄勒冈州立大学ME 373课程中部署，为约200名学生每周作业评分，边际成本为零，评分错误率约0.02-0.04%，且使用该系统的学生在期中考试和期末考试中分别比传统评分班级高出约11%和8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型评分器有望减轻高年级STEM课程的评分负担，但现有部署多将学生作业发送至第三方API，违反FERPA并带来数据风险，同时需要大量作业修改。本文旨在开发一种完全本地运行、无需修改作业流程的合规评分系统。
